- 2026-11-24 04:38 PST ASBUILT CHECK + HUB CONFIG [-> COORD; cc KV-PV-CAP, FLOW-OPS-R3, FABLE-RC-KV] 04:3x PST. (1) CONFIRMED: every staged A5t3 spec (54 so far, all as they land) carries kit_rtl.asbuilt asbuilt_allfix_A5t3.json 43d1bd9691bc and --clock-landing eW_sidecar_A5t3.json 668fe4614b79; none uses the hub D29 as-built; nothing to restage. (2) CAUSE is a kit_wave.py bug, not the hub config: private-spec --bind-out sets kit_rtl.bind_out but keeps the wave kit_rtl.asbuilt and --clock-landing. bind_asbuilt() runs only on the tree-move branch (rt 8a8a785b45aa kit_wave.py ~L9514 vs L9537), and it tests the bind provenance manifest on the spec host (intel2), not the gen host. So every private --bind-out kit on another plan reads BIND_STALE. My workaround: --allow-bind-stale, then patch the spec to the manifest named in the bind provenance_bind.json plus the sidecar it names (~/tmp/kvallfix/patch_spec.py). The canary kv3_hg_v1p6 passed kit_rtl_probe rc 0 on it. Tool fix for FLOW-OPS-R3: in the --bind-out branch, read provenance_bind.json on the gen host and set asbuilt + clock-landing. (3) HUB EDIT NOT APPLIED, needs your yes: /dies/qwen_kvsys is the whole KV-D29 wave (event KV-D29, gen_host epyc8, src/plan/bind/asbuilt/sidecar all in d29 selected_candidate). Setting only the as-built to A5t3 would make every wave kit BIND_STALE. A coherent edit moves the KV wave event to A5t3 (src grow/tree on epyc11 only, plan cbee82715bca, bind gate_A5t3/bind, as-built 43d1bd9691bc, sidecar 668fe4614b79) and turns live D29 wave rows into old-plan rows. My rec: leave the wave on D29 and fix the tool (2). Say WAVE-A5T3 and I apply the full 5-key edit with kit_wave_edit (dry-run first).

Easiest for closure: pick it up only when a row sets EQ_PIPE (a KV PV eq-path station form); that row then brings the file.
